00212: def textured_material(mat,kind):
00213:     mat.use_nodes=True
00214:     nodes=mat.node_tree.nodes
00215:     links=mat.node_tree.links
00216:     bsdf=nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00217:     if bsdf is None:
00218:         return

00220:     coord=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00221:     mapping=nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00222:     mapping.operation='MULTIPLY'
00223:     mapping.inputs[1].default_value=(24,1.4,5) if kind=="wood" else (1,1,1)
00224:     tex=nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00225:     tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value=5 if kind=="wood" else 7
00226:     tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value=3
00227:     links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],mapping.inputs[0])
00228:     links.new(mapping.outputs[0],tex.inputs["Vector"])

00028: # All rod endpoints and successive sweep points are distinct.

00035: def wood():
00036:     t = random.random()
00037:     return (.28 + .30*t, .14 + .21*t, .055 + .085*t)
00038: 
00039: def put(parent, key, node):
00040:     parent.include(key, node)
00041:     return node
00042: 
00043: def block(parent, key, size, center, color):
00044:     return put(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00045: 
00046: def bar(parent, key, start, end, width, depth, color):
00047:     dx, dy, dz = (end[i]-start[i] for i in range(3))
00048:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
00049:     center = tuple((start[i]+end[i])/2 for i in range(3))
00050:     node = box((width, depth, length), center=center, color=color)
00051:     if abs(dy) < 1e-8:
00052:         node.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx, dz)), pivot=center)
00053:     else:
00054:         node.turn('x', -math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dz)), pivot=center)
00055:     return put(parent, key, node)

00094: def plane(name, position, length=.40, width=.16, height=.13,
00095:           angle=0, metal=False, detailed=False):
00096:     a = Asset(name)
00097:     x,y,z = position
00098:     c = darkwood if metal else wood()

00101:         for side in (-1,1):

00103:                   (x,y+side*width*.45,z+height*.32), iron)

00105:               (x-.015,y,z+height*.42), iron)
00106:     else:
00107:         block(a, "wood body", (length,width,height), (x,y,z+height*.50), c)
00108:         block(a, "mouth", (.07,width*.88,.012),
00109:               (x+.015,y,z+height+.003), darkwood)
00110:     center = (x+.015,y,z+height*.93)
00111:     blade = box((.055,width*.68,height*1.05), center=center,
00112:                 color=steel if metal else iron)
00113:     blade.turn('y', -24, pivot=center)
00114:     a.include("sloping iron", blade)

00116:         put(a, "front knob", rod(radius=width*.20,
00117:             start=(x+length*.29,y,z+height*.72),
00118:             end=(x+length*.29,y,z+height*1.30), color=darkwood))
00119:         put(a, "knob top", ball(radius=width*.235,
00120:             center=(x+length*.29,y,z+height*1.32), color=darkwood))

00125:         for j,(p,q) in enumerate(((p0,p1),(p1,p2),(p2,p3))):
00126:             put(a, f"rear handle {j}",
00127:                 rod(radius=width*.115,start=p,end=q,color=darkwood))
00128:     a.turn('z', angle, pivot=position)

00414: # Initialize shader colors explicitly from SceneAct's stored finish.
00415: # Enabling nodes on a diffuse-only material can otherwise leave a default
00416: # pale Principled color, which also corrupts wood/metal classification.
00417: for mat in bpy.data.materials:
00418:     finish = tuple(mat.diffuse_color[:3])
00419:     mat.use_nodes = True
00420:     nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00421:     links = mat.node_tree.links
00422:     bsdf = nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00423:     if bsdf is None:
00424:         continue
00425:     for link in list(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].links):
00426:         links.remove(link)
00427:     r,g,b = finish
00428:     is_wood = r > g*1.23 and g > b*1.25 and r > .16
00429:     is_metal = max(finish)-min(finish) < .095 and r < .49
00430: 
00431:     # Dark stained timber and worn iron carry most of the photograph's contrast.
00432:     if is_wood:
00433:         base = (r*.66,g*.57,b*.48)
00434:     elif is_metal:
00435:         base = tuple(v*.62 for v in finish)
00436:     else:
00437:         base = finish
00438:     bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*base,1)
00439:     bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .76
00440:     bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = .55 if is_metal else 0
00441:     if is_wood:
00442:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00443:         stretch = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00444:         stretch.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00445:         stretch.inputs[1].default_value = (2.5,29,5)
00446:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00447:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 3.8
00448:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00449:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],stretch.inputs[0])
00450:         links.new(stretch.outputs["Vector"],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00451:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00452:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .17
00453:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (*[v*.65 for v in base],1)
00454:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .84
00455:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*[min(v*1.22,1) for v in base],1)
00456:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00457:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00458:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00459:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .12
00460:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .012
00461:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00462:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
00463:     elif is_metal:
00464:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .58

00027: def wood():
00028:     t = random.random()
00029:     return (0.28 + 0.30*t, 0.14 + 0.21*t, 0.055 + 0.085*t)
00030: 
00031: def put(parent, key, node):
00032:     parent.include(key, node)
00033:     return node
00034: 
00035: def block(parent, key, size, center, color):
00036:     return put(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00037: 
00038: def bar(parent, key, start, end, width, depth, color):
00039:     # Rectangular bar whose long axis lies in the wall's XZ plane.
00040:     dx, dy, dz = (end[i]-start[i] for i in range(3))
00041:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz)
00042:     center = tuple((start[i]+end[i])/2 for i in range(3))
00043:     node = box((width, depth, length), center=center, color=color)
00044:     if abs(dy) < 1e-8:
00045:         node.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx, dz)), pivot=center)
00046:     else:
00047:         node.turn('x', -math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dz)), pivot=center)
00048:     put(parent, key, node)
00049:     return node

00032: def put(parent, key, node):
00033:     parent.include(key, node)
00034:     return node
00035: 
00036: def block(parent, key, size, center, color):
00037:     return put(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00038: 
00039: def bar(parent, key, start, end, width, depth, color):
00040:     dx, dy, dz = (end[i]-start[i] for i in range(3))
00041:     length = math.sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz)
00042:     center = tuple((start[i]+end[i])/2 for i in range(3))
00043:     node = box((width, depth, length), center=center, color=color)
00044:     if abs(dy) < 1e-8:
00045:         node.turn('y', math.degrees(math.atan2(dx, dz)), pivot=center)
00046:     else:
00047:         node.turn('x', -math.degrees(math.atan2(dy, dz)), pivot=center)
00048:     return put(parent, key, node)

00299: # Open shallow basin, made entirely from explicit positive geometry.
00300: # Sloped panels leave the interior open; no Boolean cutters are created.
00301: bowl = Asset("Iron bowl filled with nails")
00302: bc = (.68,-1.08,.94)
00303: put(bowl, "basin bottom",rod(radius=.205,
00304:     start=(bc[0],bc[1],.775),end=(bc[0],bc[1],.805),
00305:     color=(.13,.15,.14)))
00306: segments = 20
00307: r0,r1 = .205,.320
00308: z0,z1 = .798,1.045
00309: slope_length = math.sqrt((r1-r0)**2+(z1-z0)**2)
00310: slope_angle = math.degrees(math.atan2(r1-r0,z1-z0))
00311: for i in range(segments):
00312:     theta = 2*math.pi*i/segments
00313:     center = (bc[0]+(r0+r1)*.5*math.cos(theta),
00314:               bc[1]+(r0+r1)*.5*math.sin(theta),(z0+z1)*.5)
00315:     panel = box((.025,2*r1*math.tan(math.pi/segments)*1.035,slope_length),
00316:                 center=center,color=(.13,.15,.14))
00317:     panel.turn('y',slope_angle,pivot=center)
00318:     panel.turn('z',math.degrees(theta),pivot=center)
00319:     bowl.include(f"sloped basin panel {i}",panel)
00320:     t1 = 2*math.pi*(i+1)/segments
00321:     put(bowl,f"rim segment {i}",rod(radius=.015,
00322:         start=(bc[0]+r1*math.cos(theta),bc[1]+r1*math.sin(theta),z1),

00387: for mat in bpy.data.materials:
00388:     if not mat.use_nodes:
00389:         mat.use_nodes = True
00390:     bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get("Principled BSDF")
00391:     if not bsdf:
00392:         continue
00393:     base = tuple(bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value[:3])
00394:     r,g,b = base
00395:     bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .72
00396:     is_wood = r>g*1.23 and g>b*1.25 and r>.16
00397:     is_metal = max(base)-min(base)<.095 and r<.49
00398:     if is_wood:
00399:         nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00400:         links = mat.node_tree.links
00401:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00402:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 3.8
00403:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00404:         tex.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .68
00405:         coord = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
00406:         stretch = nodes.new("ShaderNodeVectorMath")
00407:         stretch.operation = 'MULTIPLY'
00408:         stretch.inputs[1].default_value = (2.5,29,5)
00409:         links.new(coord.outputs["Generated"],stretch.inputs[0])
00410:         links.new(stretch.outputs["Vector"],tex.inputs["Vector"])
00411:         ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00412:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .17
00413:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = tuple(v*.65 for v in base)+(1,)
00414:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .84
00415:         ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = tuple(min(v*1.22,1) for v in base)+(1,)
00416:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00417:         links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
00418:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00419:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .17
00420:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .025
00421:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00422:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
00423:     elif is_metal:
00424:         bsdf.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = .70
00425:         bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = .48
00426:         nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00427:         links = mat.node_tree.links
00428:         tex = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00429:         tex.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 34
00430:         tex.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00431:         bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00432:         bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .19
00433:         bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .012
00434:         links.new(tex.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00435:         links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bsdf.inputs["Normal"])
